## Summary

- Extraction: 88% EXTRACTED · 12% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 21353 edges (avg confidence: 0.94)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness

- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).

## Import Cycles
- 3-file cycle: `apps/api/app/services/partnership_tasks.py -> apps/api/app/services/university_visits.py -> apps/api/app/services/partnership_universities.py -> apps/api/app/services/partnership_tasks.py`
- 3-file cycle: `apps/api/app/services/agent_applications.py -> apps/api/app/services/application_outcomes.py -> apps/api/app/services/application_offers.py -> apps/api/app/services/agent_applications.py`
- 3-file cycle: `apps/api/app/services/agent_documents.py -> apps/api/app/services/agent_orgs.py -> apps/api/app/services/agent_kyc.py -> apps/api/app/services/agent_documents.py`
- 4-file cycle: `apps/api/app/services/lead_handover.py -> apps/api/app/services/telecaller_alerts.py -> apps/api/app/services/telecaller_metrics.py -> apps/api/app/services/telecaller_leads.py -> apps/api/app/services/lead_handover.py`
- 4-file cycle: `apps/api/app/services/bdm_activities.py -> apps/api/app/services/bdm_organizations.py -> apps/api/app/services/partnership_universities.py -> apps/api/app/services/partnership_tasks.py -> apps/api/app/services/bdm_activities.py`
- 4-file cycle: `apps/api/app/services/agent_applications.py -> apps/api/app/services/application_outcomes.py -> apps/api/app/services/application_offers.py -> apps/api/app/services/agent_notifications.py -> apps/api/app/services/agent_applications.py`
- 4-file cycle: `apps/api/app/services/agent_documents.py -> apps/api/app/services/agent_students.py -> apps/api/app/services/agent_orgs.py -> apps/api/app/services/agent_kyc.py -> apps/api/app/services/agent_documents.py`
